## `ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `ALB` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Modulation-Monitor Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for FM Modulation Peak Deviation & RF Carrier Frequency Stability
- **Intro:** Modulation-Monitor Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for FM Modulation Peak Deviation & RF Carrier Frequency Stability — Pre-Calibration Safety and System Verification: RF power: rated ERP ±5% (e.g., 5 kW ±250 W). Dummy load impedance: 50 Ω ±1 Ω. RF leakage at 1 m distance: < 10 W/m².
- **Key tolerance:** RF power: rated ERP ±5% (e.g., 5 kW ±250 W). Dummy load impedance: 50 Ω ±1 Ω. RF leakage at 1 m distance: < 10 W/m².
- **Standards:** `ITU-R BS.412-9 (2019) - Limits and methods of measurement for FM`, `EN 302`, `IEC 60244-1:2016`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/ALB-6010-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)
